The community link wraps to two lines (35px) and stays as it is: it meets WCAG 2.2 AA target size, and an absolute pseudo-element on a wrapped inline did not hit-test. `tests/e2e/touch-targets.spec.ts` checks what a finger meets (`elementFromPoint` 20px above and below the centre), not the box height. _Reversible: restore the inline-flex blocks in `components-projects.css`._
- **2026-11-06** · **Without GPU compositing, every animated full-viewport CRT layer redraws the whole stack, so the rain draws on a CPU canvas and the scanline layer has no blend mode.** `MatrixRain` asks for `willReadFrequently: true`, because its 22Hz full-canvas fade cost about 100ns per backing pixel on the accelerated path (canvas-only median frame 97ms at 1280x720@1, 402ms at @2) and 4x less on CPU memory; the backing store stays capped at 2x CSS size. `.crt-scanlines` dropped `mix-blend-mode: multiply`, which equals plain compositing for a pure black gradient and only added a backdrop copy (flicker plus scanlines 54ms median frame against 19ms for flicker alone). Both are pixel-equivalent: 0 differing channels for the scanlines in WebKit and Chromium; at most 7/255 raw rain difference between WebKit raster paths. What stays costly is the animation itself: any one animated full-viewport source (noise, flicker, rain) redraws every layer, 1.2x to 2.2x cheaper in software WebKit now but still far below the reduced-motion frame rate. The options that would cut further change how an effect looks and wait for an owner choice: a 1x rain backing store, noise `normal` blend, a lower noise step rate, an adaptive frame governor. Held by `components/responsive/MatrixRain/MatrixRain.test.tsx` and `__tests__/css-paint-cost.test.ts`. _Reversible: restore `mix-blend-mode: multiply` on `.crt-scanlines` and drop `RAIN_
- **2026-11-06** · **The design-system jump links are 44px on touch; the mixer buttons and the inline list links keep their size.** `ComponentNav` chips measured 22px on a coarse pointer and now take `--spacing-target`. The mixer R/M/S/FX/EQ buttons are 24px, which meets WCAG 2.2 AA target size (2.5.8); 44px buttons would not fit five to a channel strip. The design-system home list links sit inside a sentence (`Tokens · Color palette…`), the inline exception of 2.5.8, and a 44px box there broke the list's line rhythm. Held by `tests/e2e/touch-targets.spec.ts`. _Reversible: drop the `pointer: coarse` block in `components-design-system.css`._
- **2026-11-06** · **Motion preference is a gate, not a mood: the rain and the typewriters run only while every gate is open.** The Matrix rain loop runs only when its idle start, the motion preference, tab visibility, the system-failure beat and (with a watched element) viewport entry are all open. The hero boot and the shell input placeholder stop and show static text when MOTION is switched off, restart when it is switched on, and pause while the tab is hidden or the element is off screen. Each pause source is tracked on its own, so one cannot release another. Cause: one shared `running` flag let a tab return restart the rain with motion off, and both typewriters read the preference once at mount. Held by `MatrixRain.motion.test.tsx`, `HeroBootAnimation.test.tsx`, `AnimatedPlaceholder.test.tsx`, `MatrixRain.e2e.ts` and `Hero.e2e.ts`. _Reversible: revert 20a77e9, a40a76b and 2e69829._
- **2026-11-06** · **Every demo slider takes all six slider keys from one rule, and contact requests have a deadline.** `lib/slider.ts` maps arrows, Home, End and the page keys for the fader, VU meter and knob; the VU meter is no longer a live region. `lib/post-json.ts` aborts contact and forget requests after 20 seconds, covering the body read, so a stalled connection ends in the failed-send alert instead of a permanent "sending". _Reversible: revert 3d5a655 and 82cdc8c._
- **2026-11-06** · **The sitemap states no `lastModified`.** Its dates were hand-written per route and `CONTENT_UPDATED_AT` was never set, so every lastmod was stale; search engines ignore lastmod that is not accurate. A git-derived date was rejected: a shallow build clone dates every file at the deploy commit, which is just as wrong, and a route-to-files map is another list to keep in sync. Held by `__tests__/sitemap.test.ts`. _Reversible: restore the date constants, or derive dates from a full-history build._
- **2026-11-06** · **Standalone links are 44px touch targets on coarse pointers, and the project card yields padding, not type size, at 320px.** A review found the back-to-top button at 40px and card-header, measured-heading, community and CV company links at 16 to 35px. `--to-top-size` now references `--spacing-target`. Standalone links get `min-height: var(--spacing-target)` under `pointer: coarse`; inline company links keep their 28px `::before` hit area. `ORIGINATION_PLATFORM_UI` broke as `…_U / I` at 320px because the card padding left 212px for 221px of text. The type-scale gate holds card titles at 16px, so the card padding drops to `--spacing-3` below 360px and `text-wrap: balance` handles narrow desktop windows. Held by `tests/e2e/touch-targets.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/to-top-clearance.spec.ts` and `tests/e2e/projects-mobile.spec.ts`. _Reversible: delete the `pointer: coarse` blocks and the narrow-phone media query; the small targets and the orphaned `I` return._
- **2026-11-06** · **pt copy states the same facts as en, in Portuguese.** `/pt/summary` used the English-only `profileLocation`, `/pt/cv` kept "Brazil" and "formerly Daitan" and dropped the hospital-platform detail, project stats kept English tokens, and decimals mixed `.` and `,`. Pt now uses `localizeDecimals` (`content/profile.ts`) for the magnitudes it shows. The pt About status reads "vagas de nível [Sênior] … remoto". Held by `content/locale-parity.test.ts`. _Reversible: revert the content commit; the English fragments return on the pt pages._
- **2026-11-06** · **An unknown address sends the 404 page in its first bytes: the catch-all throws `notFound()` inside a Suspense boundary whose fallback is the page.** `[...missing]/page.tsx` called `notFound()` with no boundary, so Next prerendered the route's fallback shell as `<html id="__next_error__">` with an empty body (1.0 KB, status 404, no lang, title or content until hydration). `components/ErrorPages/MissingRoute.tsx` throws inside `Suspense` with `NotFoundPage` as the fallback (10 KB shell, status 404, the group's `<html lang>`). That exposed `usePathname()` in `GlobalMotion` on a route with unknown params, so `RootShell` wraps `GlobalMotion` in `Suspense fallback={null}`. The not-found metadata adds `robots: { index: false }` and drops the canonical, which had pointed a 404 at the home page beside `index, follow`. Hydration replaces the fallback DOM with the boundary output, so tests that click or hold links on the 404 page wait for `waitForHydration` first. Held by `__tests__/missing-route.test.tsx` and the 404 cases of `tests/e2e/html-lang.spec.ts`. _Reversible: restore the one-line `notFound()` pages and drop the Suspense wrappers; unknown URLs return to the empty shell._

- **2026-11-06** · **The ask budget counts every token a request used: the input reservation is derived from the system prompt, and settlement charges usage above it.** The fixed `RESERVED_INPUT_TOKENS = 2200` was sized for a 1500-token prompt; the prompt is now 11,860 characters, and `settleBudget` returned early whenever usage exceeded the reservation, so every cache-cold request under-counted the monthly cap. `ASK_RESERVED_INPUT_TOKENS` is `ceil((SYSTEM_TEXT.length + ASK_MAX_QUESTION_CHARS) / 3) + 300`, and `BudgetStore.chargeOverage` adds the difference (the eval store ignores it, since it bounds request count). Held by `__tests__/budget-cap.test.ts`. _Reversible: restore the constant and the early return; the counter under-counts again._
- **2026-11-06** · **Approved error, loading and receipt states ship as built.** A crashed island keeps its box and shows `! component unavailable` with one `[ RELOAD ]`; decorative islands pass `fallback={null}`. The 500 page prints boot lines with real elapsed seconds and an ellipsized copyable ref, and `app/error.tsx` lazy-loads it so the error chunk stays out of every route's first load. Server reads of the ask and Lighthouse metrics stop at 3s (`withTimeout`, `SSR_REDIS_TIMEOUT_MS`), and the Upstash client retries once after 50ms (`REDIS_RETRIES`, `REDIS_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`): with the env vars unset, the default retry policy stalled each command about 4s and cut SSR at the 10s function limit, which turned every island into the crash fallback on previews. Held by `lib/with-timeout.test.ts` and `__tests__/redis-bounded-retry.test.ts`. _Reversible: revert the state commits._
- **2026-11-06** · **Rate-limit buckets are per IPv6 /64 and the JSON routes refuse cross-site posts.** `getClientIp` reduces an IPv6 address to its /64 (a client owns the whole prefix, so per-address buckets reset at will), maps IPv4-mapped addresses to IPv4 and sends non-IP values to `unknown`. `defineHandler` answers 403 `forbidden_origin` before the rate limit when `Sec-Fetch-Site` is not `same-origin` or `none`; a missing header passes, with no `Origin` fallback to avoid false refusals behind a proxy. A `hashIp` failure is a 503 `storage_unavailable` inside the envelope, and every 429 carries `Retry-After`. _Reversible: revert the two helpers; no data migration._
- **2026-11-06** · **`/api/log` stores at most 500 records a day across all clients (`rl:errlog:day`).** Past the cap it answers ok and drops the record; a failed check records anyway. 10 per minute per client alone allowed about 14,400 records of up to 20 KB a day into the Redis shared with contact messages. Error reports send origin plus path, never the query or fragment. _Reversible: raise the cap in `lib/rate-limit.ts`._
- **2026-11-06** · **Every workflow runs on a read-only token by default.** The repository default token is write, so `ci.yml` and `mutation.yml` declare `permissions: contents: read` at the top; `__tests__/scripts/workflow-permissions.test.ts` fails for any job without permissions or with `write-all`. _Reversible: delete the top-level block; a job that needs more declares its own._
- **2026-11-06** · **`/api/ask` stops the model call and still settles when the reader goes away.** The response stream had no cancel handler, so a closed tab made enqueue and close throw before settlement and logging ran: the reservation stayed charged, no log row was written, and the call ran to the 30s timeout. Cancel now aborts the upstream signal, the pump races a cancel signal, and the log row carries status `aborted`. Held by `__tests__/ask-client-disconnect.test.ts`. _Reversible: remove the cancel handler and the `aborted` status._
- **2026-11-06** · **Token accounting follows the AI SDK v7 usage contract, and the ask eval meters real cost.** `inputTokens` is the total prompt size and the cache split is a breakdown of it; `countAskTokens` counts cache tokens once. The eval grades only completed streams (a gateway error is `errored`, so an outage cannot pass a jailbreak item), takes `featureCostUsd` from the route's settled usage, and uses the one calibration gate in `lib/eval/calibration.ts`. Held by `lib/ask/__tests__/usage.test.ts` and `__tests__/ask-eval-harness.test.ts`. Open: the system prompt is probably under Haiku 4.5's 4096-token cache minimum, so its `cacheControl` marker may be inert; one live reading of `cacheCreationTokens` decides. _Reversible: restore the sum and the constant estimate._
- **2026-11-06** · **Preview deployments read a separate Redis.** Production keeps its own `UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL/TOKEN` (Production only). Preview and Development read a free Upstash database (`erikunha-preview-redis`) connected through the Vercel integration, which sets `KV_REST_API_URL/TOKEN`; `hasRedisCredentials()` in `lib/rate-limit.ts` accepts either complete pair (never a mix) and is the one check used by `getRedis`, the ask route log gate and the eval publisher. - **2026-11-06** · **`SectionNavigationProvider` keeps its context value fixed; current section, drawer and motion live in an external store.** The scroll observer changed `currentId` in the context once a section was in view, so React abandoned hydration of the contact form and shell below it: server DOM discarded, typed text and focus lost, island repainted. The context now holds the store, actions, locale, copy and chrome; `useCurrentSection`, `useDrawerOpen` and `useMotionOn` read the store with `useSyncExternalStore` (server snapshot is the initial state). With the DOM kept, `ContactForm` and `InteractiveShell` adopt text typed before hydration in a layout effect; a capture module that saved the text around the discard was rejected as a workaround of this cause. - **2026-11-06** · **No context value may change above an unhydrated island.** `BreakpointProvider` changed its value after hydration on viewports under 768px (server snapshot desktop, client mobile); a context change above a Suspense boundary that has not hydrated forces it to client-render, so the lazy shell fell back to its loading box and regrew about 300ms later, and inputs typed meanwhile reset. `useBreakpoint` is now a plain `useSyncExternalStore` hook over `matchMedia` with no provider, and `TerminalChromeBar` renders both label variants and hides one in CSS so its height is the same before and after hydration. - **2026-11-06** · **An early contact submit stays on the page, and measured boxes keep one height.** The contact `<form>` carries `method="dialog"`, so a submit before the island hydrates does nothing instead of a native GET that put the name, email and message in the URL (and so in history and logs); `onSubmit` takes over once hydrated. Held by the `contact form before it hydrates` case in `tests/e2e/contact-states.spec.ts`. The ask eval error box fills a hidden, `aria-hidden` copy of the tile block and reserved labels place their text in the first grid cell, so loading, data and error share one height (held by `tests/e2e/layout-stability.spec.ts`). _Reversible: remove the attribute and the `.measured-ask-body` wrapper._
- **2026-11-06** · **The global error page ships a shell and lazy-loads its body.** With two root layouts the global-error entry is its own chunk group, so Turbopack copied every module it imported into a second first-load chunk (about 3.6KB gzipped on every route). `GlobalErrorPage` imports only `site-locale` and `server-error-core` and renders a static 500 message with retry and home; `GlobalErrorBody.client.tsx` adds the boot lines and reference block on demand, and a failed body chunk resolves to the same static message. `reportClientError` lives in `lib/report-client-error.client.ts` and the 500 copy is split into `server-error-core` and `server-error-detail`. `/` went from 164.6KB to 161.9KB against 164.7KB, the client sum from 220.2KB to 216.9KB against 220KB. Held by `__tests__/global-error-chunk-failure.test.tsx` and both JS gates. _Reversible: revert the split; the gates fail again by about 50 bytes._
- **2026-11-06** · **Each locale has its own root layout, so `<html lang>` is right on every route.** `app/(en)` (`/`, `/cv`, `/summary`, `/design-system/*`, `/links/en`) renders `lang="en-US"` and `app/(pt)` (`/pt/*`, `/links`) renders `lang="pt-BR"`, both through `components/RootShell.tsx`; URLs are unchanged and the pt wrapper `div lang` workaround is gone. Each group has its own `error.tsx` (one above the root layouts would render a document without html and body) and its own `[...missing]` catch-all with `not-found.tsx` inside the catch-all segment; `global-not-found.tsx` (experimental) measured about 9 KB gzipped on every first load and a layout-level `not-found.tsx` 1.8 KB, so neither is used. - **2026-11-06** · **The contact copy table stays out of client chunks.** `content/contact-copy.ts` built both locales at module load, a call a bundler cannot drop, so `ContactForm` and the lazy `ContactTimezone` island each shipped the full table (4.0KB gzipped, twice) for one helper. The Zod-free helpers and limits (`fillCopy`, `contactFieldError`, `contactHoursPhrase`, `CONTACT_LIMITS`, `CONTACT_EMAIL_PATTERN`) live in `content/contact-rules.ts`, imported by client modules, `lib/contact-validation.ts` and the route; the table is read only on the server. Held by `pnpm bundle-check`. The measured error box names its real cause: the approved 3s line only for a `ReadTimeoutError`, "could not be read" otherwise. - **2026-11-06** · **Missing Upstash credentials fail at once.** Without `UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL` or `_TOKEN`, `getRedis()` returns a client whose commands reject with `RedisNotConfiguredError`, never a synchronous throw, so `.catch` fallbacks hold; tests that mock `Redis.fromEnv` stub both vars. - **2026-11-06** · **Shell limits are measured, never invented.** `/api/ask` 429 sends `Retry-After` in whole seconds from the limiter reset and omits it when the reset is unusable; the shell counts it down and states no duration without it. The idle timeout is 15s of silence between chunks and ends with a retry that resends the same question. Offline shows a fixed, empty-collapsing `role=status` notice. _Reversible: drop the header; `SHELL_LIMITS.requestTimeoutMs` sets the timeout._
- **2026-11-06** · **The contact ref is the request id.** `/api/contact` stores `contact:msg:<requestId>`, the id returned to the visitor and printed as `Ref:` in the email, and `/api/log/forget` deletes that key and its outbox entry besides the ask logs, answering known and unknown ids alike. The queued copy states the real schedule (daily cron, `CONTACT_MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS` attempts), held to `vercel.json` by a test; the board's "daily for 7 days" was not true. The receipt uses the site's one reply window instead of the board's "2 business days". _Reversible: yes._
- **2026-11-06** · **One owner per repeated mechanism.** `ExternalLink` / `externalLinkProps` own the `target="_blank"` and `rel="noopener noreferrer"` pair; `Panel` owns the `.panel` frame for About and Contact; `PageMain` owns `MAIN_CONTENT_ID`; `serializeJsonLd` (`lib/json-ld.ts`) is the only way JSON enters a `<script type="application/ld+json">` and escapes `<`, U+2028 and U+2029; `deferProps` owns the deferred-section class and marker; `useCopyFeedback` (`lib/use-copy-feedback.client.ts`) owns clipboard state for every copy button (resets after 1500ms; failures persist unless `resetOnFailure`); `lib/typewriter.ts` (`runPhraseLoop`, `typeOut`) is the one type, hold and erase loop for the shell placeholder, boot dialog, mobile boot and RoleTyper, each keeping one pending timer; `LazyIsland` (an `ErrorBoundary` around `Suspense`) wraps every `React.lazy` island; `buildPageMetadata` (`app/_lib/build-metadata.ts`) is the one openGraph and twitter builder; `tests/e2e/_helpers/geometry.ts` owns the viewport list and the overflow check. ## 2026-11-05: UX review findings on layout shifts, contact states, language switch, CV print and back-to-top

- **2026-11-05** · **Late states reserve their room instead of moving the page, and the contact, language, print and back-to-top findings close with tests.** Measured before the fix: the shell grew 60px while thinking (811 to 871 at 390, 570 to 630 at 1280) because the Stop row appeared between feed and prompt, so Stop now sits inside the input row; Measured grew 19px at 390 when its data resolved, so each block ends in a `MeasuredStatus` slot sized to its longest status copy (data included, which leaves a blank line when data is present); the timezone host moved -12 to +30px on mount (worst in pt) because the static box carried one hand-set height per width, so `.ctz-host` now takes a per-locale `--ctz-host-min` for nine width bands, taken from the tallest live panel over five zones and three clock times (a visitor whose zone has no overlap sees up to about 100px of empty panel at the bottom, the price of never growing). A failed send keeps growing the form by the alert and its gap: the summary renders above the fields, takes focus and scrolls into view, so it is the thing being read, not something it pushes. The contact submit row gets one polite `role="status"` line (`sending…`, marked fields, failed send) instead of an `aria-live` wrapper around the button. The timezone box says it is loading (hidden without scripting or after a chunk failure) and a `<noscript>` line names the email where the form is missing. The header language switch appends the section in view on the two home pages after hydration and stays a plain link elsewhere and without JS. In print the CV certifications and education share one key column, badges read as words (`credentialBadges`) and the hire link is dropped. Footer text keeps the back-to-top reach clear on phones. Held by `tests/e2e/layout-stability.spec.ts`, `contact-states.spec.ts`, `locale-switch-position.spec.ts`, `cv-print.spec.ts`, `to-top-clearance.spec.ts` and the unit tests beside each component. _Reversible: restore the `.shell-progress` row, drop the slot and the `--ctz-host-min` bands, and revert the copy fields._

## 2026-11-05: review fixes for gates, scripts and the forget route

- **2026-11-05** · **Site-voice copy and accessible names follow the page language, and the scroll position line is decorative.** `CompanyLink`, `CompanyMention` and `BrandLink` take a required `locale`, so the new-tab disclosure and the brand name read in Portuguese on `/pt` (`content/company-link.ts` and `content/brand.ts` are now per locale; the `/links` and design-system chrome stay English with their chrome). The Portuguese hero label is `AGORA:`, the Portuguese 404 says `TENTE ISTO` and titles itself `404 · erikunha.dev`, the budget refusal reads `AI answers are paused for this month. Email … instead.` (Portuguese twin in `content/ask-errors.ts`) and the shell renders the address as a `mailto:` link, the Measured unavailable text says what to do next without the word aggregate, the Portuguese jailbreak tile reads `RESISTÊNCIA A JAILBREAK`, and the Measured status line is `role="status"`. The position line is now `aria-hidden` and carries no `progressbar` role, because the browser scrollbar already conveys the position and a named control outside every landmark failed the axe `region` best practice; this supersedes the 2026-11-02 choice to keep it as the one named progress control, and `positionLabel` leaves the navigation copy. Machine voice (shell commands, boot lines) stays English. Held by the tests beside each change, and `tests/e2e/navigation.spec.ts` for the line. _Reversible: restore `role="progressbar"`, `aria-label` and `aria-valuenow` on `.pos-line` plus `positionLabel`, and make `locale` optional on the three components; no stored data depends on any of it._
- **2026-11-05** · **`/links` and `/links/en` read as one phone column (K1) and as a `1fr : 1.618fr` desktop pair (K5), and the stack line and launch teasers follow the data.** Owner approved K1 for phones and K5 for desktop. Below 768px the order is chrome bar, identity (avatar, name, tagline, host, stack), the 2-column pill grid of 48px rows (the copy-link button is the last cell, so five linked pills and the button fill three even rows), latest video, history, playlists, then the QR from 600px, then the footer; the stacks become `display: contents` so the QR sorts after the playlists without a second DOM. From 1024px the left column holds identity, pills and QR and the right holds the feed, split by `--phi-pair-columns-minor-first` (measured 355.7 : 575.5px at 1024 and 427.1 : 691.0px at 1280, ratio 1.618). The tablet band (768 to 1023.98px) keeps the two columns but splits `1fr : 1fr`: at the golden ratio the left column would be 258px at 768 and stay under the 280px readable floor until about 826px, while 1:1 gives 344px at 768, 370px at 820 and 471px at 1023, so one ratio holds across the whole band and the 2-column pills (168px cells at 768) never need a 1-column fallback. The pills left the footer for the identity column, so `LinkPills` owns them and `LinksFooter` keeps only the portfolio link. The stack line is the full `profileStack.all` joined with ` · ` (wrapping inside the identity box, no overflow at 320) and `profileStack.short` is removed, since nothing else read it. The "first videos landing soon" status and its `statusLabel` are removed from the schema and content, and the "next video / soon" ticker renders only when the feed has no latest video, so the empty state is unchanged. The reduced-motion ticker end-to-end check goes: with the fixture feed the ticker never renders, so `Ticker.test.tsx` holds its markup and `tests/e2e/links.spec.ts` holds its absence. `tests/e2e/links-layout.spec.ts` holds the column counts, the 1.618 ratio, the order, the stack-line wrap and the no-overflow check at 320, 390, 820 and 1280. _Reversible: restore `LinksFooter` pills, the three-column footer grid, `profileStack.short`, the `status` and `statusLabel` fields and the unconditional `Ticker` from git history; no stored data depends on the layout._
- **2026-11-05** · **`/summary` and `/pt/summary` read in one screen: header, a 4-up stat strip, the call-to-action row, then projects on the wide side of a 1.618 : 1 grid and tech stack plus contact on the narrow side.** Owner approved variant U1. The grid is `--phi-pair-columns` from 1024px. At 768px the narrow column measured 258.6px, under the 260px floor that keeps the chip rows to two lines, so 768 to 1023.98px stacks the two columns (the narrow column is 356px at 1024px). Under 768px the page is one column, the stats stay 2x2 as on the home hero and the buttons are full width. The stack is two chip rows read from `profileStack.core` and `profileStack.ai`, the AI row with a lime border. Print keeps the grid and hides only the reading bar, the skip link and the action row. Held by `tests/e2e/summary-layout.spec.ts` and `__tests__/reading-views.test.tsx`. _Reversible: restore the `.reading-grid` rules and the `TerminalPanel` project list in `components-reading.css` and `SummaryPage.tsx`; no data migration._
- **2026-11-05** · **`parseClaudeVerdict` strips backtick code spans and pairs `**` per line, and a compare file list at the API cap of 300 never counts as baseline-only.** A quoted glob such as `app/api/**` in a review overview paired its `**` with the verdict's opening `**`, so the gate read no verdict while the workflow guard passed. The compare endpoint truncates its file list at 300 entries, so a list that long can hide a non-baseline file behind baseline PNGs. Held by `__tests__/scripts/check-claude-approval.test.ts` and `__tests__/scripts/check-claude-approval.exec.test.ts`. _Reversible: restore whole-body span pairing and drop `COMPARE_FILES_CAP`; the guard test in `__tests__/scripts/claude-review-verdict-guard.test.ts` then needs its code-span case removed._
- **2026-11-05** · **Every script entry guard compares `import.meta.url` with `realpathSync(process.argv[1])`.** Node resolves the symlink behind a main module for `import.meta.url` but leaves `argv[1]` as typed, so a script started through a symlink exited 0 without running. Held by `scripts/__tests__/main-module-guard.test.ts`, which scans `scripts/`, and a symlink run in `scripts/__tests__/check-section-order.test.ts`. _Reversible: drop `realpathSync` from the guards; nothing else depends on it._
- **2026-11-05** · **`/api/log/forget` reads its body through `withBodyLimit` at 1024 bytes, and `/api/csp-report` stays uncapped because it never reads its body.** The payload is one UUID, so the cap leaves wide headroom. Held by `__tests__/ask-log-persistence.test.ts`. _Reversible: unwrap `withBodyLimit` in `app/api/log/forget/route.ts`._
- **2026-11-05** · **No model review runs unless the owner asks, and `DECISIONS.md` is excluded from Biome formatting.** The `pr-review-orchestrator` skill and claude-review are both opt-in, and `CLAUDE.md` says so in one place. The log is append-only history, and Biome's Markdown formatter rewrites emphasis in old entries, which would turn every line into a diff. _Reversible: delete the `!DECISIONS.md` entry in `biome.json` and reformat once._

## 2026-11-05: the language switch, footer map, guitar module and responsive audit

- **2026-11-05** · **Status dots blink while motion is on: the MOTION dot in the topbar and the menu, and the timezone panel's online dot.** Owner: the dots read as live when they pulse. One class, `.status-dot-blink`, reuses the `live-blink` keyframe (1.6s, two steps, well under the three-flashes-per-second limit of WCAG 2.3.1) and stops when MOTION is off (`body[data-motion="reduce"]`), which is also the pause control WCAG 2.2.2 asks for. The offline timezone dot stays still. All three dots render one `StatusDot` component (`components/StatusDot/`) with one `.status-dot` class: a square 6px dot, size and color set per use through `--status-dot-size` and `--status-dot-color`, so a change to the shape lands on every dot at once. The mixer's chain and plugin dots and the badge dot are square too (sharp-corner rule); only the mixer knob stays round, as a dial. Held by `components/StatusDot/StatusDot.test.tsx` and `tests/e2e/status-dots.spec.ts` (blink, and a 0px radius on the MOTION dot). _Reversible: drop the class from the three dots, or render plain spans again._
- **2026-11-05** · **A delivered contact message shows a receipt: its request ID with a copy control, the send time, and a deletion request link.** Owner picked the R7 receipt so a visitor never needs devtools to find the ID. The link opens an email to Erik with the ID in the subject instead of calling `/api/log/forget`, because that endpoint deletes only the ask logs (`ask:log:*`), not stored contact messages; a button that called it would claim a deletion that never happens. The ID line truncates with an ellipsis and keeps the full value in `title` and in the copy. Held by `components/client/ContactForm/ContactForm.test.tsx` (receipt in en and pt, the mailto carries the ID, no receipt without an ID). _Reversible: drop `ContactReceipt` and the `success.receipt` copy._
- **2026-11-05** · **The Clean Architecture claim for the independent consulting role is retired from every published surface.** Owner: it described added complexity rather than a result worth citing. The project description, the SEO keyword list, the ask eval answer and `llms.txt` drop it; the role, dates and metrics stay. Held by `__tests__/retired-claims.test.ts` (projects, Person schema, CV JSON-LD, the ask system prompt, the eval corpus and `llms.txt`). _Reversible: restore the four strings and delete the test._
- **2026-11-05** · **The language switch lives in the header as an EN/PT button pair (`LocaleSwitch`).** Owner: "TIRE O EN English PT DO MENU E DEIXE NO HEADER TAMBEM". `LanguageSwitch` (exported from `components/responsive/SectionNavigation`) is the one component for both bars: the desktop topbar renders it right after HIRE, in the left group (menu trigger, section links, MOTION, VIEW_CV, HIRE, language switch, then a gap and the brand icon alone at the right end), and the mobile title bar renders it centred between the menu trigger and the brand. It keeps the 2026-08-30 contract: links carrying `hrefLang` and `aria-current="page"`, never a toggle, rendered only where the current address exists in both languages (`languageSwitchPaths`). The drawer renders no language links. It renders `LocaleSwitch` (`components/LocaleSwitch/LocaleSwitch.tsx`), the same pair the `/links` chrome bar renders: two design-system buttons, the current language filled (`primary`) and the other outlined (`secondary`), 44px at every width, no separator. Owner: the /links switch style everywhere; one component means the three surfaces cannot drift apart again. Held by `components/LocaleSwitch/LocaleSwitch.test.tsx`, `components/responsive/SectionNavigation/SectionNavigation.test.tsx`, `__tests__/desktop-topbar-nav.test.tsx` (focus order and visibility at 768 to 1440), `components/responsive/MobileTitleBar/MobileTitleBar.test.tsx` and `tests/e2e/section-navigation.spec.ts` (no language links in the drawer, header placement, PT from `/` lands on `/pt` and back). _Reversible: move `LanguageSwitch` out of the two bars; no content or route changes._

- **2026-11-05** · **`/cv`, `/summary` and their `/pt` twins use the home header: `AppShell` with the desktop topbar, the mobile title bar, the dock and the one menu, and the page-local `reading-bar` is gone.** Owner: the reading pages carry the same header as the home page, with the real page locale (`BrandLink`, `siteNavChrome`, the skip label all follow `en` or `pt`; `/links` still renders English chrome). `components/reading/ReadingShell.tsx` wraps both pages in `AppShell`, then `.reading-view`, then `main#main-content.page-container.page-main`, so the header lines up with the home page and the body keeps its U1 and CV measure inside `.reading-doc`. `headerLanguageSwitchPaths` now returns the cv and summary pairs, so the header `LocaleSwitch` replaces the bar's switch; only `/links` and `/links/en` keep a page-owned switch. The back link is the brand icon, and PRINT / SAVE PDF stays in the call-to-action row in the page body. Print hides the shell: one `@media print` block in `components-reading.css` removes the skip link, both bars, the dock, the menu, the position meter, the to-top button and the CRT layers, hides the rain canvas with `visibility` (its inline `display` wins over any rule), zeroes the dock clearance padding that print inherits from the narrow page width, and removes the container padding, so `/summary` still prints on 1 A4 page and `/cv` on 3. The shell adds client islands to both routes: first-load JS rises from 143.3 KB to 148.8 KB, under the 164.7 KB gate. `shared.skipLabel`, `shared.navLabel` and `shared.nav` leave the reading-views content and schema because nothing renders them. Held by `tests/e2e/reading-shell.spec.ts` (the topbar and title bar match the home header by element and by pixel at 1280 and 390, the switch is centered, one h1 and one main and one skip link and no banner, print hides every shell part and the PDF page counts), `tests/a11y/axe.spec.ts` (the four routes at desktop and 390), `__tests__/reading-views.test.tsx` and `__tests__/locale-alternates.test.ts`. Rejected: keeping a page-local bar that copies the shell (it drifts, as the three headers did before 2026-11-03), and `display: none !important` on the canvas (`visibility: hidden` does the same job without an override). _Reversible: render the two pages without `ReadingShell`, restore the `reading-bar` markup, its CSS and the three `shared` content fields from git history, and add `/cv`, `/pt/cv`, `/summary` and `/pt/summary` back to `PAGE_OWNED_LANGUAGE_SWITCH`._

- **2026-11-05** · **The footer site map is folder rows over ruled link lists, an accordion on phones, the one exception to the 2026-07-12 "nothing collapses" rule.** Owner decision. The card title keeps `LS -R ~/` over a divider, then each folder is a full-width row: the directory, its file count (`2 files`) and, on phones, a `+`/`-` sign. Links hang off a vertical rule instead of the `├──` connectors, and a faint divider separates the folders. Below a 38rem container the rows are native `<details name="footer-sitemap">`, one open at a time, with `./work/` open on load (`treeOpenDirectory`, checked by the schema against the group list). From 38rem all folders show in three columns, and from 68rem in six. Native `<details>` over a client button: zero JavaScript on every route, keyboard and screen reader behaviour from the platform, and it works with JavaScript off. The links render twice (grid and accordion) and the container query hides whichever does not apply, so only one set is ever visible or in the accessibility tree. The phone footer measures 1683px at 390x844, against about 1830px before. The disclosure ban in `__tests__/module-section-visibility.test.ts` now exempts exactly `Footer.tsx` and `components-footer.css`, and a second test fails if those files use a disclosure token outside the `sm-fold` accordion, or if an exemption goes unused. Page sections still never collapse. Held by `Footer.test.tsx` and `Footer.e2e.ts` (exclusive opening from `./work/`, counts, 44px rows and links under a coarse pointer, columns per width, no-JS navigation). _Reversible: drop the `sm-folds` block and its CSS, remove the two exemptions, and regenerate the footer baselines._

- **2026-11-05** · **Responsive audit: the desktop mixer moves its plugin chain to a second row below 1280px, type-reveal headings opt out of the attribute hydration check, and non-interactive `tabindex=-1` focus targets paint no outline.** The desktop mixer is served to every non-phone user agent (iPad included), so at 834px its eight-column row left 29px per plugin chip; a second row keeps one mixer markup instead of a tablet variant. `TypeReveal` mutates class and style before streamed islands hydrate, so `suppressHydrationWarning` on the marked elements is the opt-out React documents for an intentional pre-hydration mutation; deferring the effect was rejected for lack of a reliable all-hydrated signal. Section and 404 headings receive programmatic focus for announcement only, so the browser ring (off-palette `auto` blue) is removed for `tabindex=-1`; keyboard rings on interactive controls are unchanged. Reversible: each is one CSS rule or attribute.

- **2026-11-05** · **Section drawer rows shrink to 36px (`--spacing-target-pointer`) only at 1024px and up with a fine pointer; touch keeps 44px.** At 1280x720 every drawer row at 44px pushed the footer links below the fold; WCAG 2.5.8 asks 24px and fine pointers do not need the touch floor, so the row height is a per-input token instead of one size. Group titles move to the muted tertiary tone and the item labels step up to `--text-sm`, so the current item is the only lime-bordered element in the list. Rejected: shrinking the type to fit (it was already at the 12px floor). Reversible: delete the `pointer: fine` override.

- **2026-11-05** · **The guitar rig and the DAW mixer are one module at `sec-guitar`, topped by the animated EQ spectrum strip, and the mixer shrinks to three channels.** The module reads, top to bottom: the chain label and EQ strip, the `SIGNAL_CHAIN.LIVE` panel with the INPUT, AMP and OUT cards, the details beside the 20rem live photo with the camera labels above it, then the mixer. The standalone `sec-daw-mixer` leaves the page, registry, nav group, footer and CSS order map, so the registry has nine destinations and `DEFERRED_SECTION_COUNT` is four. The mixer keeps CH 01, CH 02 and MASTER: with the strip already showing the lead chain, five guitar tracks repeated it at a cost of about 1,500px of scroll. Mobile now renders all three channels (the old two-card slice hid MASTER), and the "N GTR + MASTER" counts derive from the channel list. The module header is `CAT ~/.GUITAR_RIG + ./MIX --LIVE`, the nav and footer name it "Guitar and mixer", and the `mixer`, `daw` and `mix` aliases resolve to it. Held by `GuitarSection.test.tsx`, `DawMixerContent.test.tsx`, `content/schemas.test.ts` (mixer `.length(3)`), `content/section-registry.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/guitar-mobile.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/mixer-mobile.spec.ts` and `tests/e2e/deferred-modules.spec.ts`. _Reversible: restore the standalone `DawMixerSection`, its registry entry, nav group, footer link and CSS order entry; the signal chain schema and CSS do not change._

- **2026-11-05** · **T8 a11y sweep: four real fixes, three documented deferrals, no gate movement.** The C1-M4 audit predated merges #341-#346, so the sweep re-derived every finding from the current tree; axe was already green, so the pass was manual (keyboard walk, landmarks, targets, reflow, i18n of accessible names, reduced motion). Fixed: (1) below the md breakpoint the desktop DAW console now scrolls locally (`.daw-mixer-desktop` gets `overflow-x: auto` under 767px and the rows a 720px min-width) because the 392px of fixed grid columns squeezed channel faders to 2x24px and VU meters to 15x24px, a hard WCAG 2.5.8 failure for desktop UAs at 320 to 767px; mobile UAs were never affected (they ship the static card variant). (2) The mobile StatusBar is decorative device chrome and is now `aria-hidden`: its `role="status"` live region announced the clock on every 15 second tick, and `aria-label="device status"` was English on `/pt`. (3) `ToTopButton` and the DesktopTopbar nav took accessible names from hardcoded English; both now read locale copy (`back to top`/`Voltar ao topo`, `Site navigation`/`Navegação do site`) via two new `NavigationCopy` fields, and the to-top scroll honors the motion preference (instant under reduce). Deferred with reason: `/pt` keeps `<html lang="en">` with the pt-BR wrapper div (the 2026-08-30 pattern; every visible node including the chrome sits inside the wrapper, so language is programmatically determinable and axe stays green); inline text links stay 16 to 18px tall (WCAG 2.5.8 inline-link exception); mixer slider labels stay English musical vocabulary on both locales. Held by the StatusBar and ToTopButton unit tests, the `tests/e2e/mixer-mobile.spec.ts` narrow-window console test, the `tests/e2e/locale-routes.spec.ts` pt chrome test, and the axe suite. _Reversible: restore `role="status"`, the literal labels, and `overflow-hidden` on the mixer, and drop the two copy fields._

- **2026-11-05** · **Hero stat tile labels wrap below 480px instead of painting into the neighboring tile, and no stat tile text may overflow its tile at 320px in either locale.** The design sweep caught the pt labels "ativos em fundos", "MAU e-commerce" and "deslocamento de layout" colliding across the 2x2 mobile tile borders at 320 and 375: `.hero-stats .stat-tile-label` was `white-space: nowrap` at every width, so the longer pt labels overflowed their `minmax(0, 1fr)` tiles. `app/css/components-hero.css` lets the label wrap at every width (`overflow-wrap: anywhere`, no `nowrap`): a later review found "deslocamento de layout" painting 40px past its tile at 1280 too, so a width-scoped `nowrap` only moved the defect; EN labels still fit on one line. The value line stays `nowrap`. Held by `tests/e2e/hero-layout.spec.ts` (stat tile text stays inside its tile at 320 and 1280 on `/` and `/pt`, measured by Range text rects against the tile box, the element box alone stays clamped and hid the overflow). _Reversible: delete the media query; the collision returns below 480px._

- **2026-11-05** · **The p95 ask-eval tile label is localized per dictionary: "P95 LATENCY" in EN, "LATÊNCIA P95" stays in pt.** `content/measured.ts` hardcoded the pt label for both locales, so the EN measured tiles read "LATÊNCIA P95" at every width. The label joins the same per-locale ternary as the sibling metric labels and the `AiMetricsSection` rendering path is unchanged. Held by `content/measured.test.ts`. _Reversible: one line restores the hardcoded label._

- **2026-11-05** · **The desktop DAW table gains a paint-level no-overlap regression test at 1280 and 320, and the narrow-mixer overlap itself is resolved by the 720px min-width console scroll shipped in #350.** The sweep caught the eight-column grid fr tracks collapsing at 320 so wrapped channel names painted over the GAIN knob. #350 keeps every column at its desktop width inside a 720px-minimum, locally scrollable console (overflow-x-auto below md), preserving 24px fader and meter targets; this branch keeps the guard, `tests/e2e/mixer-mobile.spec.ts` asserting that no two cells of a channel row overlap at 1280 and 320 with text paint checks (deferred modules revealed so geometry is live, clipping ancestors honored, trailing letter-spacing trimmed, 4px horizontal tolerance against the neighbor cell edge plus a line-leading tolerance vertically: the guard targets the defect class, tens of pixels on the sweep build, not sub-letter grazing), so the collapsed-track defect class cannot return. A restack of the row below 768px was implemented and set aside during the rebase onto #350: the two mechanisms conflict by construction (the min-width rule already targets `.daw-channel-row`), and the merged scroll console wins. Noted for any future rework: Biome 2.6.2 `noInvalidGridAreas` false-positives on multi-row areas strings with tokens longer than one character, and explicit `min-content` grid mins re-arm the nowrap description min-content that `min-w-0` exists to suppress. _Reversible: delete the extended test; the #350 console scroll stands alone._

- **2026-11-05** · **The dock hire item drops its arrow glyph: "HIRE" and "CONTRATE", with the dock icon already an arrow.** The pt dock item wrapped at 320 ("CONTRATE ↗" over two lines, caught by equal item heights hiding it from the old height-only assertion); the dock cells are too narrow for eight characters plus the arrow. `HIRE_SHORT_LABEL` keeps the arrow for the topbar and a new `DOCK_HIRE_LABEL` in `content/nav.ts` feeds only the dock. Held by `tests/e2e/mobile-reflow.spec.ts` (one-line dock labels at 320 on `/` and `/pt`, the pre-existing equal-height assertion kept) and `__tests__/hire-action-labels.test.tsx` (topbar and dock labels now read their own canonical constants instead of one shared literal). _Reversible: point the dock item back at `HIRE_SHORT_LABEL`._

## 2026-11-05: claude-review is opt-in and no longer a merge requirement

- **2026-11-06** · **The browser install retry kills the whole process group, waits four minutes per attempt, and the e2e-functional job gets 25 minutes.** The first version killed only the `pnpm` process on timeout. The `apt-get` it had started kept running and held the dpkg lock, so attempts two and three failed at once with apt exit 100. The child now starts in its own process group and the timeout sends SIGKILL to the group. Held by `__tests__/scripts/install-playwright-browsers.test.ts`, which also checks that the job budget leaves ten minutes for tests after every attempt. _Reversible: drop `detached` and the group kill, and restore the 15-minute job timeout._
- **2026-11-06** · **CI installs Playwright browsers through `scripts/install-playwright-browsers.mjs`: three attempts of three minutes each.** Twice in one day the webkit e2e job spent its whole 15-minute budget inside `playwright install --with-deps webkit`, fetching apt packages from a slow Azure mirror at seconds per package, and was cancelled before a single test ran. A bounded retry turns a stalled mirror into a fresh attempt and leaves the job time to run its tests. Held by `__tests__/scripts/install-playwright-browsers.test.ts`, which also fails on any bare `playwright install` step in a workflow. _Reversible: restore the three `pnpm playwright install --with-deps` steps in `ci.yml`._
- **2026-11-06** · **`@lhci/cli` moves to `^0.16.0` (Lighthouse 12.6.1 to 13.6.0), and the audit ignore list is deleted.** Root cause of the ignores: lhci 0.15 pulled `lighthouse@12` > `puppeteer-core@24` > `@puppeteer/browsers` > `extract-zip` (two advisories) and `chromium-bidi@14` > `zod@3` (one). lhci 0.16 pulls `puppeteer-core@25` and `@puppeteer/browsers@3`, so `extract-zip` and `zod@3` are gone from the lockfile and `pnpm audit --audit-level=moderate` reports no vulnerabilities with `pnpm.auditConfig` removed. **Guard:** `__tests__/audit-ignore-scope.test.ts` now fails on any `ignoreGhsas` entry (red while the three ids existed, green after), replacing the lockfile-closure argument that only an ignored advisory needed. **Lighthouse 13 removed the legacy audits** (`uses-text-compression`, `uses-responsive-images`, `font-display`, `render-blocking-resources`, `dom-size`, `uses-long-cache-ttl` and others); lhci 0.16 maps the ids in `lighthouserc.json` and `lighthouserc.mobile.json` onto their insight audits (`dom-size` reports as `dom-size-insight`) and states the mapping is removed in a future version. `pnpm lhci` and `pnpm lhci:mobile` pass against a production build with warnings only, as on 0.15.1, and every measured category is 1.0 on desktop and mobile. Moving the config to insight ids is a separate change, because the option semantics differ per insight. _Reversible: pin `@lhci/cli` back to `^0.15.1` and restore the three ids in `pnpm.auditConfig.ignoreGhsas` and the closure test from the commit before this change._
- **2026-11-06** · **jsdom moves to `^31.0.0`.** The 31 breaking changes were checked against the suite and none needed edits: the optional canvas backend changed to `@napi-rs/canvas` (no test installs a canvas package; `HTMLCanvasElement.prototype.getContext` is stubbed in `__tests__/matrix-rain-advanced.test.tsx`), HTML parsing now pauses at blocking scripts (no test builds a `JSDOM` directly; Vitest owns the environment), and CSS parsing and computed styles use a new implementation (all 1389 unit tests pass unchanged, and the computed-style cases that jsdom cannot evaluate stay in Playwright per `STANDARDS.md`). The engine floor is `^22.22.2 || ^24.15.0 || >=26.0.0`, unchanged from 30. _Reversible: pin `jsdom` back to `^30.1.3` and reinstall from the lockfile before this change._
- **2026-11-06** · **Playwright moves to `~1.65.0` for `@playwright/test` and `playwright`.** The 1.65 breaking changes were checked against this repo and none needed code or config edits: class fields now follow `useDefineForClassFields` (no page-object classes with parameter properties exist), actions wait for non-inert elements (no `inert` in the specs), cookie matching follows RFC 6265 and `params` append to the URL query (neither API is used), `test.skip(condition, description)` keeps working (the three call sites run unchanged), and Node 20 is no longer tested (the repo needs 22.22.2 or newer). The `chromium` and `chromium-mobile` projects pass against the committed darwin baselines with no regeneration, so Chromium 158 renders the captured sections identically. `webkit-desktop` and `webkit-mobile` fail three `visual.spec.ts` cases (hero on mobile, hottest takes on both) against their darwin baselines, and the same three fail on 1.64.1 with the same baselines, so the bump did not cause them. _Reversible: pin both packages back to `~1.64.1` and reinstall from the lockfile before this change._
- **2026-11-06** · **Stryker moves to 10.1 (`^10.1.1` for core and the vitest runner), and a kill canary fails any run where mutation testing cannot observe a mutant.** Root cause: `@stryker-mutator/vitest-runner` 9.6.1 runs each mutant against only its covering tests by building a `testNamePattern` from `describe test` names joined with a space. Vitest 5 matches that pattern against names joined with ` > `, so the pattern matches nothing, every mutant run executes zero tests (`testsCompleted: 0` in the report) and is recorded `Survived`: 0 killed of 30 on `lib/links/relative-time.ts`. Reproduced in a clean two-file fixture as a 2x2: Vitest 4.1.12 with runner 9.6.1 kills 12 of 12, Vitest 5.1.0 with 9.6.1 kills 0 of 12, Vitest 5.1.0 with 10.1.1 kills 12 of 12. In this repo Vitest 4.1.12 with 9.6.1 kills 26 of 30 and Vitest 5.1.0 with 10.1.1 kills 26 of 30, so the earlier "0 of 30 under Vitest 4 too" did not reproduce. Runner 10.1.1 picks the separator from the installed Vitest version. **Why the gate read green:** the weekly guard in `mutation.yml` only required one scoreable mutant, and a survived mutant is scoreable; the job is also `continue-on-error`, so a 0% score posted to the summary and nothing failed. **Guard:** `scripts/check-mutation-kills.mjs` fails a report in which any covered mutant survived with `testsCompleted === 0` (the runner filtered every test out) or in which nothing was killed. It judges the full report in the weekly job, and `pnpm check:mutation-canary` runs Stryker on `lib/links/relative-time.ts` with no incremental file (so a recorded result cannot mask the break) in the `test` job on pushes to main and on PRs that change `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `stryker.config.mjs`, `vitest.config.ts`, `tests/mocks/` or the canary itself, about 10 seconds. Red against Vitest 5.1.0 with runner 9.6.1 (`30 covered mutant(s) survived without a single test running`, exit 1), green with 10.1.1 (26 of 32 scoreable killed). Pure verdicts are held by `__tests__/scripts/check-mutation-kills.test.ts`. **Options weighed:** (A) bump Stryker, chosen because the fix is upstream and the version pair stays supported; (B) keep 9.6.1 behind a `pnpm.patchedDependencies` separator patch, rejected because the patch must be re-derived on every runner release; (C) hold Vitest at 4, rejected because it reverts the Vitest 5 upgrade to keep an unsupported pairing. A cross-provider review of the options returned no usable findings (one provider out of quota, two without repository context), so the choice rests on the reproduced 2x2. _Reversible: revert the bump and delete the canary; mutation runs then silently read 0% again under Vitest 5._
- **2026-11-06** · **Vitest moves to 5.1 with `@vitest/coverage-v8` and `@vitest/ui` on the same version.** Vitest 5 takes `vite` as a required peer (this repo already declares it directly) and needs Node 22.12 or newer. The v5 breaking changes that could reach this repo were checked and none needed code or config edits: `clearMocks` now defaults to true (no test depends on call history surviving between tests), coverage `include`/`exclude` match without picomatch `contains` (the covered file set and thresholds are unchanged), and nested `vi.mock` now throws (none exist). `@stryker-mutator/vitest-runner` 9.6.1 declares `vitest >=2.0.0`, so it stays. _Reversible: pin the three packages back to `^4.1.12` and reinstall with the lockfile from before this change._
- **2026-11-06** · **The audit ignore list grows to three ids, each tied to a vulnerable version range that the lockfile proves is dev-only.** New advisories landed against transitive dev tooling; every one with a release was fixed by raising an override floor or adding `vite` as a direct devDependency (an override never reaches a peer, and vitest takes vite as a peer). Two have no fix in reach: `GHSA-7pqw-9j4j-h8q3` is a second extract-zip advisory (no patched release, same lhci path as `GHSA-jmr9-qjv8-65gv`), and `GHSA-mrhx-c7xc-m3w5` hits `zod@3`, which lighthouse pulls through `puppeteer-core > chromium-bidi@14` and which cannot move to zod 4 under that API. The direct zod moves to `~4.6.7`, above the fixed version. `__tests__/audit-ignore-scope.test.ts` now walks the lockfile by version and fails if any version an ignored advisory covers is reachable from `dependencies`; a mutation raising the zod threshold above 4.6.7 reds it. _Reversible: drop the two new ids and the override bumps; the audit gate goes red until lhci moves._
- **2026-11-06** · **Dependencies take ranges, never exact pins: `^` by default, `~` where a minor breaks this repo.** `scripts/check-dep-pinning.mjs` rejects a bare version (a prerelease build such as `@typescript/native-preview` excepted, since no range matches dev builds) and requires `~` for `TILDE_REQUIRED`: next and @next/mdx (a minor moves the framework floor and the polyfill checksum), react and react-dom (the floor), zod (inference), typescript (Stryker's API, held on 6.x), biome (2.6 added Markdown and YAML formatting and new lint rules), playwright (rendering moves every visual baseline), shiki and rehype-pretty-code (highlight output in the design-system pages). Held by `__tests__/scripts/check-dep-pinning.test.ts`. _Reversible: restore the exact versions and the old ALLOWED regex._
- **2026-11-06** · **Biome formats code, not prose: Markdown and YAML are excluded from the 2.6 formatter, and `noComponentHookFactories` is off in test files.** Biome 2.6 started formatting `.md`/`.yml`; reformatting every doc on main would rewrite tables that the open redesign integration branch edits heavily, so prose formatting is a separate change after that merge. The new React rule flags `vi.mock` factories that return stub components; a module mock is created once per module evaluation, so the rule's premise (a component redefined every render) does not hold there. _Reversible: drop the two `overrides` entries in `biome.json`._
- **2026-11-06** · **The MCP route runs on MCP SDK v2 through mcp-handler 2.** `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` 1.x is gone; `@modelcontextprotocol/server` supplies `McpServer`, tool `inputSchema` is a `z.object`, and `maxDuration` moved from the handler options to the route segment export. mcp-handler 2 serves Streamable HTTP for the 2026-07-28 spec and falls back for 2025-era clients; it no longer reads the path, so the fixed route `app/api/mcp/route.ts` answers `/api/mcp` and no dynamic segment exists. Held by `__tests__/agent-surfaces.test.ts`. _Reversible: pin mcp-handler 1.x and restore the sdk import._
- **2026-11-05** · **`claude-review.yml` triggers only on an `issue_comment` carrying `/claude-review`, and merge readiness is CI green, base contained and `pnpm pr:gate`; `pnpm claude-gate` stays as an optional command.** Owner decision: the review must be requested, not run on every push, and must not block a PR. The check was never a required status check (the ruleset requires `quality-fast`, `typecheck`, `test`, `build`, `detect-changes`, `ci-gate`), so it blocked only through the documented `claude-gate` step. On large diffs each push produced a new round that surfaced a few more findings, and every push cost a run. The `pull_request` event is gone from the workflow, so its event-specific paths went with it: the skip for PRs that edit the workflow, the `track_progress` and `prompt` inputs, and the base-ref checkout (the root is now the default branch). The comment path, the author fence, the concurrency group, the prompt materialisation and the verdict assertion are unchanged. Reversibility: restore the `pull_request` trigger and its event branches in the workflow and the predicate and guard tests, and put `pnpm claude-gate` back in the merge-readiness lines of `CLAUDE.md` and `docs/handbook/review-merge-release.md`.

## 2026-11-05: a Playwright-only change arms the browser jobs

- **2026-11-05** · **`tests/` is an `app` pathspec and `tests/visual/` a `ui` one in `scripts/detect-changes-paths.mjs`.** Neither list named `tests/`, so a PR touching only Playwright specs or baselines set `app=false` and `ui=false`, and e2e-functional and e2e-visual skipped, which GitHub counts as passing. The PR that fixed the red `/links` spec merged its own spec unrun. Held by `scripts/__tests__/detect-changes-paths.test.ts`. _Reversible: drop the two entries; spec-only PRs then skip the browser jobs again._

## 2026-11-04: bilingual routes, chrome pass, CI runner pin and golden-ratio sections

- **2026-11-04** · **Playwright browser caches are keyed by the runner image version.** A webkit cache entry created on an `ubuntu-26.04` runner was restored on `ubuntu-24.04` jobs, and MiniBrowser died at launch with `libicudata.so.78: cannot open shared object file`, failing every webkit e2e job that drew the poisoned entry (exit 127, `browserType.launch: Target page, context or browser has been closed`). Each CI job that caches `~/.cache/ms-playwright` now records the hosted-runner `ImageVersion` into `$GITHUB_OUTPUT` first and includes it in the `actions/cache` key and restore-keys, so a browser build can never be restored across runner images. The poisoned entries were also deleted from the repo cache. _Reversible: remove the `Runner image version` step and drop the version segment from the cache keys._

- **2026-11-04** · **Home section column pairs settle on the golden ratio, as tokens.** Measured at 1280px: hero bio:terminal 1.500, about columns 1.500 in the 1024 to 1279 band, footer session:netstat cards 1:1.15, while guitar, contact and /links already sit at 1.618 via `--phi-pair-columns`. The arbitrary pairs move to phi: the hero terminal takes the minor share of a phi flex pair (new `--phi-pair-flex-minor`, `0 0 calc((1 - var(--phi-inv)) * 100%)`, so bio:terminal becomes 61.8:38.2), the about grid 1024 to 1279 band reads `--phi-pair-columns` instead of `1.5fr 1fr`, and the footer cards read the new `--phi-pair-columns-minor-first` (minmax 1fr then 1.618fr) instead of `1fr 1.15fr`. A `<flex>` value cannot appear inside `calc` (Chromium drops `calc(1.618 * 1fr)` entirely), so the minor-first pair is a whole track list token next to `--phi-pair-columns`, not a computed ratio. The about grid keeps its deliberate 1:1 split from 1280px up, mobile stacking is untouched (every change lives inside desktop media), and the career log, the DAW channel strip, the hottest takes and projects grids stay as designed: their columns are content sized, not ratio sized. The contact section keeps its literal `minmax(0, 1.618fr) minmax(0, 1fr)` because `ContactSection.css.test.ts` parses the fr numbers to assert the ratio; a token swap there is pixel identical and would only churn the test. Hero and footer baselines of `tests/visual/visual.spec.ts` change with the new widths. _Reversible: restore `flex: 0 0 40%`, `1.5fr 1fr` and `1fr 1.15fr`; no markup depends on the ratios._

- **2026-11-04** · **CI and smoke workflows pin `runs-on: ubuntu-24.04` instead of `ubuntu-latest`.** Evidence gathered from the runner fleet: every failing `e2e-visual-chromium` job in the 2026-11-04 runs landed on the `ubuntu-26.04` image (rollout of the new runner image under the `ubuntu-latest` label) and rendered the committed Linux baselines off by about one pixel (for example a 1054 vs 1055 footer), while every green run's visual jobs ran on `ubuntu-24.04`, the image the baselines were regenerated on; the same drift broke next-build cache digests with `digest-mismatch` errors. Pinning the CI and smoke jobs to `ubuntu-24.04` makes the environment deterministic again without touching assertion strictness, so the visual gate keeps its exact pixel semantics. _Reversible: set `runs-on: ubuntu-latest` back in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and `smoke.yml`, and regenerate the Linux baselines once the fleet converges on a single image._

- **2026-11-04** · **Site-wide bilingual pt-BR/EN ships with the bare address serving English and pt-BR additive under `/pt`: the address alone determines the language, and the scheme extends the /links refusals rather than its address assignment.** The five 2026-08-30 bullets bind this feature: no i18n framework, no catalogue, no negotiation, no middleware; the bare `https://www.erikunha.dev/` keeps returning a page because the distributed CV PDF embeds it; each language names itself canonical with `alternates.languages` plus x-default; client islands receive the active locale's strings as props; three Portuguese-vacuous filters were fixed in the same change; the ask endpoint keeps one cached byte-identical system prefix with the answer language carried in the user turn. What those bullets left open, which language the bare address serves, is decided here as **English**, for three recorded reasons. First, the ADR's own reversibility note says "the second language is additive; deleting the second locale's routes and content restores a single-language site", which is only true when the existing site stays put; bare=pt would relocate every English URL and make deletion restore nothing. Second, the bare-address traffic is the CV QR traffic, and the CV is English, so the traffic the constraint exists for gets the language it reads. Third, zero existing canonical, OG URL, sitemap entry, footer link or external backlink moves, so no migration risk accrues anywhere. pt-BR is reached at `/pt`, `/pt/cv` and `/pt/summary`; x-default points at the English bare address; `/links` (pt) + `/links/en` keep their 2026-08-30 addresses untouched, and `/design-system` stays English-only this milestone, so pages without a pt counterpart render no language switch. The switch (links with `hrefLang` + `aria-current="page"`, never a toggle, per the /links decision) lives in the header for shelled pages and in the reading bar for `/cv`/`/summary` and their pt twins, and it renders nowhere on `/design-system`. The root `<html lang>` stays `en`; every `/pt` page wraps its subtree in `lang="pt-BR"`, the same shape /links ships, so screen readers switch voice (axe and Lighthouse stay green on both). The 404 renders the default locale, because an unmatched address carries no locale a single prerendered not-found could know. Machine voice stays English on both locales: shell commands and their local outputs, the hero boot lines, dmesg, the DAW mixer track names/plugins/transport and the EQ signal strip, matching the /links precedent for shell chrome (its `shellTitle`/`historyCommand`/`qrCommand` are already locale-neutral), while everything a visitor reads as site voice carries the translation; so do all aria-labels. Machine-readable surfaces stay English per 2026-10-30: the ask system prompt, the Person/ProfilePage JSON-LD, `llms.txt`, `/api/erik.json` and the eval corpus all read the `en` copy of the now-bilingual content records, and the identity gates (NOW_CURRENTLY, degrees-in-llms.txt, talks sentence) were re-pointed at that canonical English with pt assertions added beside them. The content layer carries both languages as `Record<SiteLocale, T>` parsed by the existing schemas in every module (`content/site-locale.ts` holds the locale model, path mapping and hreflang pairs), and the prop threading moves `InteractiveShell`, `ContactForm`, `ContactTimezone`, the drawer/dock/topbar/position meter and the footer reboot/halt chrome onto props picked server-side: `content/navigation.ts` and `section-registry.ts` stay zod-free for the client, and the picked `Navigation` crosses the boundary as one prop. The headroom constraint held: `/` and `/pt` measure an identical 163.3 KB total (31.4 KB app-owned against the 32.8 KB budget); the two routes byte-identical client JS is the empirical proof that both languages never ship in one chunk, since the pt copy rides the RSC payload the per-route gate does not measure. The three filters now cover Portuguese: `INJECTION_RE` takes pt override phrasings and accented role tokens, `LEAK_MARKERS` carries the six pt translations of the prompt fragments it guards, and `metric-consistency.test.ts` gained pt metric keywords (`latência`, `carregamento`, `conversão`, `build desktop`) plus the pt stat-label keys so the sign sweep and the tile-to-receipt map cover the pt projects copy. Held by `tests/e2e/locale-routes.spec.ts` (both locales at 320 and 1280: render, no redirect on the bare address, lang attribute, canonical, alternates, switch link with `aria-current`), the axe scans of `/pt`, `/pt/cv` and `/pt/summary`, `__tests__/locale-alternates.test.ts`, and the re-pointed identity/metric/hire gates. _Reversible: delete `app/pt/`, the pt columns and the switch; every existing English URL is untouched, so the site degrades back to single-language by deletion alone. Known follow-ups, recorded not hidden: the shell chrome on `/links` stays English this milestone (its page-local switch already serves the pair); localizing it is a follow-up; the design-system docs are untouched; pt aliases were added to the drawer search but the ask corpus stays English per the ADR._

- **2026-11-04** · **Owner chrome pass: the shell drops `ls`/`face`/`hire`, toolbar chips fill the prompt instead of submitting, the drawer loses its section-count badge, the drawer close button matches the other drawer controls, and the menu trigger moves to the left of the brand in both topbars.** Owner decisions, one pass. (1) Shell: `ls`, `face` and `hire` leave `content/shell-ui.ts` and `content/shell-commands.ts` (enum members, suggestions, commands and canned responses), so those inputs now fall through to the LLM like any unknown text; `help` stops listing them; `cat skills.md` answers with the 2026-11-03 resume headline. Clicking a toolbar chip fills the prompt with that text and focuses it instead of submitting (Enter still runs it); `clear` keeps running immediately and the AI flow is untouched, because a chip that fires a network request on one tap surprised the owner. (3) Drawer: the `10` section-count badge is removed entirely (markup, `.menu-badge` CSS, `sectionCountLabel` in `content/navigation.ts` and `NavigationCopySchema`), and `.menu-close` stops being the one 700-weight control in the panel: it takes the motion-row typography (`--text-xs`, 400, 0.1em, `--font-mono`), the same 44px `--spacing-target` height as every other drawer control. (4) Topbars: `MenuTrigger` renders first and the brand slot last, in DOM order so focus order matches visual order; `aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`, the 44px targets and the focus-return path are unchanged. Held by the InteractiveShell, SectionNavigation, MobileTitleBar and desktop-topbar-nav tests and `tests/e2e/section-navigation.spec.ts`. _Reversible: restore the enum members, responses and chip `runCommand`, the badge markup and copy key, the 700-weight close rule, and move `MenuTrigger` back after the brand in the two topbars._

- **2026-11-04** · **The documentation is re-synced to the redesigned terminal chrome and current facts: role targeting reads Senior and Tech Lead, the registry counts ten destinations, the design-system inventory reads ten primitives with nine RSC, and the docs state the merged post-change chrome (full-width guitar signal strip with the camera HUD, uploads-playlist `/links` history, and the landing owner-chrome batch: the `ls`/`face`/`hire` shell commands removed, the drawer section-count badge gone, menu-left topbar with an actions-first row and the brand at the right, and the EQ curve badge top-left on the live photo).** A full-tree documentation audit found 60 stale claims across 26 files (wrong counts, reversed Mermaid edges, pre-redesign descriptions); every fix derives from the current source, not from an older doc's wording, and the audit's in-flight items are documented to the post-change truth. _Reversible: this changes prose only; reverting the docs PR restores the previous wording._

- **2026-11-04** · **Career descriptions render in the signal green and the community block fills the section width, per the owner type review.** The T14 mobile type audit of the career, contact and hero surfaces (reconstructed after the scratch findings file turned up empty) plus two owner follow-ups landed in one CSS-only pass. `app/css/components-about.css` sets the hero context meta to the `--text-xs` step below 390px, replacing a `calc(var(--text-sm) - var(--border-width))` that produced a 13px size outside the `--text-*` scale. `app/css/components-career.css` renders `.career-body` in `--color-primary-500`: on the black module surface the pair measures 15.4:1, so the standing no-signal-body rule (written for pairs that fail WCAG AA) does not apply, and the axe gate keeps passing. The same file drops the 72ch cap on `.career-community` so the community bullets and status line use the section width instead of wrapping inside a half-empty column at desktop; the long-form `.career-body` keeps its 72ch measure. `app/css/components-hero.css` deletes the dead `.hero-stats .stat-tile-value` and `.stat-tile-label` color and font-size declarations, which the `StatTile` utilities had been overriding since the design-system component landed, keeping only `white-space: nowrap`; the rendered sizes were already the component's (`--text-xs` mobile value, `--text-base` desktop value). `app/css/components-contact.css` deletes the dead `#sec-contact > header h2` font-size override, which the Module utilities layer had always beaten, moves `.contact-success` to `--border-width` and `--spacing-6`, and routes the three control-glow shadows through a new `--shadow-glow` token. Held by `tests/e2e/type-scale.spec.ts` (hero context step, career body color, community width) plus the untouched hero, contact and layout suites. No captured visual-regression section changes: the hero snapshot runs at desktop widths only and the contact computed values are identical. _Reversible: one revert restores every prior value from git history._

- **2026-11-04** · **The `/links` history fills with regular videos by paging the channel uploads playlist when `YOUTUBE_API_KEY` is set; without the key the feed stays RSS-only, exactly as before.** The feed's newest 15 entries hold about two regular videos because the rest are Shorts, so the five-row history (`history -5` in `content/links.ts`) ran two rows deep. With the key, `getChannelFeed` reads the channel uploads playlist in pages of 50, at most four pages, stopping early once five regular videos survive the same two Shorts filters (a `/shorts/` URL, then a duration of 60 seconds or less from per-page `videos.list` enrichment), which costs at most nine quota units per cache miss inside the existing 900 second stale cache. When the playlist cannot be read or holds no regular video, the feed falls back to the RSS feed's 15 entries, where the key still applies the duration filter; without a key nothing changes: RSS only, URL-only Shorts filter, zero config. The rendered history changes only where the key is set, so live verification happens on the Vercel deployment. Reversible: delete `fetchDeepUploads` and restore the single uploads page read.

## 2026-11-03: resume-driven content, header brand, timezone panel and the /links feed

- **2026-11-03** · **The site now also signals the AI direction the owner is steering toward, on facts only.** The Infnet MBA entry gains an optional `focus` line rendered in the /cv print view and the ask prompt: coursework (ML, deep learning, LLMs, RAG, multi-agent systems) plus the current self-study list, agent orchestration with LangGraph and LLM observability with LangSmith and Langfuse. The ask Targeting section states the direction (AI-native product engineering, agentic systems, LLM observability, MBA in progress) and the eval corpus gains a matching factual case, so the LLM answers career-direction questions with the same line. The hero-adjacent `NOW_CURRENTLY` line stays equal to the canonical profile summary (an identity-consistency gate), so the inference lives only in education and the stated direction, per the owner request to hint lightly. _Reversible: drop the optional focus field and the corpus item; every renderer guards its absence._

- **2026-11-03** · **The site content and the downloadable CV now follow the 2026-11-03 resume, and the "12-agent AI system" claim is de-emphasized across surfaces.** The owner asked for less focus on the 12-agent framing: the hero's fourth stat becomes "CLS → 0" (the Raylu A/B migration number from the new CV), the Betsson project card leads with the reviewable-engineering-system fact instead of the agent count, the hottest take and the unknowns line keep the spec-driven point without the number, and the ask corpus, the calibration answer and the ask system prompt state the same de-numbered fact. `public/erik-cunha-cv.pdf` is replaced with the new resume file under the same name so every existing link keeps working; the career log itself already matched the new CV (Raylu since Jun 2026, Betsson Mar 2025 to May 2026). The hero and hottest-takes baselines change with the new copy. _Reversible: restore the previous PDF from git and the prior copy in each content file; no schema or structural change was made._

- **2026-11-03** · **The timezone panel treats Erik's call window as 10:00 to 18:00 São Paulo and picks the best slot at the earliest round half hour inside both windows, not the midpoint.** Owner correction: Erik takes calls from 10:00, so `lib/timezone/overlap.ts` splits the shared window constant into `ERIK_WORK_START_HOUR` 10 and `VISITOR_WORK_START_HOUR` 9; the visitor keeps 09:00 to 18:00 as the assumed office day, since the owner's statement covered Erik only. The old slot landed on the overlap midpoint, which for a visitor two hours behind rendered "12:30 you · 14:30 erik" and read as a bug; the slot now rounds the overlap start up to the next half hour and fails closed to no slot when no round half hour fits, so the same visitor sees "09:00 you · 11:00 erik" and a same-zone visitor sees 10:00 both sides. Zones east of Dubai lose their sliver of overlap because 10:00 São Paulo is past their evening, and the panel's existing "no live window, async only" state covers them. Held by `lib/timezone/overlap.test.ts` and `components/client/ContactTimezone/ContactTimezone.test.tsx`; the contact section baselines of `tests/visual/visual.spec.ts` change with the rendered hours. Landed in 244fe5a. Reversible: restore a single shared window constant and the midpoint line in `computeOverlap`.

- **2026-11-03** · **The claude-gate carries an Approve across baseline-only commits: `evaluateGate` takes the changed-file list and no longer fails a review as STALE when the only commits after the reviewed SHA touch visual baseline snapshots.** The defect the loop tripped on #329: the approval landed on `0563abe`, the follow-up commit added regenerated linux baselines, and the gate read "reviewed SHA is not HEAD" although the diff since the approval was images only, so the loop spent a `/claude-review` request re-approving what the owner rule already said to carry. The gate now fetches the GitHub compare between the reviewed SHA and the head and carries the Approve when every changed file is a PNG inside a `*-snapshots` directory; any other file in the diff fails closed as STALE exactly as before, and a reviewed SHA that is not an ancestor of the head (checked via the compare merge base) fails closed too, which keeps the force-push case on the old side of the line. _Reversible: drop the `changedFiles` parameter and the compare call; STALE-for-any-drift returns._

- **2026-11-03** · **`/links` shows regular videos only, in a 16:9 frame, in one monochrome treatment.** Shorts are dropped in `getChannelFeed` (a `/shorts/` URL before the duration lookup, a duration of 60 seconds or less after it) so history and the latest card never lead with a vertical clip. Every cover sits in a `links-thumb` frame at `aspect-ratio: 16 / 9` with the image filling it: YouTube's 4:3 `hqdefault` carries its own black bars, so a 16:9 frame trims only the bars, where the earlier fixed 104 and 280 pixel heights cut real picture at most widths. A grayscale image under a multiplied `--color-primary-500` layer keeps differently coloured covers inside the page palette; the cover colour returns on hover and focus. The candidate read stays at the feed's 15 entries, so the history can hold fewer than five rows when most uploads are Shorts. Reversible: drop the two filters and the `links-thumb` class.

- **2026-11-03** · **`/links` and `/design-system` run inside the home shell, one site menu serves all three, and page rhythm steps through the phi tokens.** The two pages had their own headers (a ticker with a window bar on `/links`, a text site bar with a second drawer on `/design-system`), so a visitor moving home to links to design system met three different headers and, on the design system, two menus. `AppShell` now wraps all three, with a `drawerExtra` slot that carries the design-system page tree inside the one menu; the old `SiteBar`, its drawer and its CSS are deleted. The brand icon link (the `brand` slot of `AppShell`) goes home from every page, `LINKS` joins the topbar navigation and the menu, `DESIGN_SYSTEM` and `LINKS` carry `aria-current="page"` on their page, and the dock marks no item as current off the home page.
